	}

		original_tag = ALB

		ALB = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_leader = { character = ALB_esad_toptani } }
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
					elections_allowed = no
					name = ALB_house_of_toptani_party
					long_name = ALB_house_of_toptani_party
				}
				effect_tooltip = { ALB_esad_toptani = { custom_effect_tooltip = ALB_albanian_serbian_union_tt } }
				hidden_effect = {
					ALB_esad_toptani = {
						set_character_name = ALB_esad_toptani_prince
						add_country_leader_role = {
							promote_leader = yes
							country_leader = {
								ideology = absolute_monarchy
								traits = { ALB_fulfilled_ambitions }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = { add_political_power = 100 }
			set_country_flag = monarchist_puppet
			hidden_effect = { ALB = { get_current_government_type = yes } }
			SER = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = ALB
					autonomous_state = autonomy_tgwr_real_union
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { ALB = { restore_previous_government_type = yes } }
			set_portraits = {
				character = ALB_esad_toptani
				civilian = {
					large = "gfx/leaders/ALB/ALB_esad_toptani_civilized.png"
					small = "gfx/interface/ideas/ALB/ALB_esad_toptani_civilized.png"
				}
				army = {
					large = "gfx/leaders/ALB/ALB_esad_toptani_civilized.png"
					small = "gfx/interface/ideas/ALB/ALB_esad_toptani_civilized_army.png"
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = swap_ALB_statuti_organik_i_shqiperise
			add_to_variable = {
				tooltip = political_power_factor_tt
				ALB_sos_political_power_factor = 0.05
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ALB_country_in_disarray }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ALB_statuti_organik_i_shqiperise }
				set_variable = { ALB_sos_war_support_factor = ALB_as_war_support_factor }
				set_variable = { ALB_sos_political_power_factor = ALB_as_political_power_factor }
			}
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		modifier = {
			factor = 30
			original_tag = ALB
		}
	}

joint_focus = {
	id = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs
	icon = GFX_ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs
	text_icon = joint_ALB_TUR_focus_style
	cost = 5
	search_filters = {
		FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY
		FOCUS_FILTER_RESEARCH
	}
	prerequisite = { focus = ALB_ottoman_material_support }
	x = 1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = ALB_ottoman_material_support
	available = { ALB = { NOT = { has_war_with = TUR } } }
	completion_reward_joint_originator = {
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = ALB }
			custom_effect_tooltip = modify_ALB_country_in_disarray
			add_to_variable = {
				tooltip = stability_factor_tt
				ALB_sos_stability_factor = 0.1
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ALB_illiterate_society }
						has_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs1
						has_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs2
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = {
								var = ALB_is_research_speed_factor
								value = -0.05
								compare = greater_than_or_equals
							}
							has_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs1
						}
					}
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ALB_illiterate_society }
					set_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs1
				}
				else = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = modify_ALB_illiterate_society_tt
					add_to_variable = {
						tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
						ALB_is_research_speed_factor = 0.05
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
					set_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs2
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = TUR }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_TUR_crippling_illiteracy_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
				TUR_ci_research_speed_factor = 0.02
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
							check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
							check_variable = { TUR_albanian_catholic_state_variable < 0 }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_target_albanian = -0.3 }
					set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_compliance_growth_albanian = 0.05 }
				}
				#for tooltip
				TUR_set_old_albanian_value = yes
				set_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = old_albanian_value }
				divide_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_albanian_population_factor_focus = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				add_to_temp_variable = { TUR_albanian_population_factor_focus = -0.1 }
				multiply_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = TUR_albanian_population_factor_focus }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = old_albanian_value }
			}
			add_to_variable = { TUR_albanian_population_factor_new = -0.1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs_tt
		}
	}
	completion_reward_joint_member = {
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = ALB }
			custom_effect_tooltip = modify_ALB_country_in_disarray
			add_to_variable = {
				tooltip = stability_factor_tt
				ALB_sos_stability_factor = 0.05
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ALB_illiterate_society }
						has_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs1
						has_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs2
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = {
								var = ALB_is_research_speed_factor
								value = -0.05
								compare = greater_than_or_equals
							}
							has_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs1
						}
					}
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ALB_illiterate_society }
					set_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs1
				}
				else = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = modify_ALB_illiterate_society_tt
					add_to_variable = {
						tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
						ALB_is_research_speed_factor = 0.05
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
					set_country_flag = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs2
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = TUR }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_modify_TUR_crippling_illiteracy_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				tooltip = research_speed_factor_tt
				TUR_ci_research_speed_factor = 0.02
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = empty_space
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { TUR_albanian_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
							check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
							check_variable = { TUR_albanian_catholic_state_variable < 0 }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_resistance_target_albanian = -0.3 }
					set_variable = { TUR_ottoman_compliance_growth_albanian = 0.05 }
				}
				#for tooltip
				TUR_set_old_albanian_value = yes
				set_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = old_albanian_value }
				divide_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_albanian_population_factor_focus = TUR_albanian_population_factor_new }
				add_to_temp_variable = { TUR_albanian_population_factor_focus = -0.1 }
				multiply_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = TUR_albanian_population_factor_focus }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs = old_albanian_value }
			}
			add_to_variable = { TUR_albanian_population_factor_new = -0.1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs_tt
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		modifier = {
			factor = 30
			original_tag = ALB
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 5
			original_tag = ITA
		}
	}
}

joint_focus = {
	id = ALB_utilize_turkish_intelligence
	icon = GFX_TUR_empower_the_special_organization
	text_icon = joint_ALB_TUR_focus_style
	cost = 5
	search_filters = {
		FOCUS_FILTER_FRA_OCCUPATION_COST
	}
	prerequisite = { focus = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs }
	x = 1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = ALB_albanian_turkish_cultural_programs
	available = {
		TUR = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				has_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa
			}
			else = { has_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar }
		}
	}
	available = { ALB = { NOT = { has_war_with = TUR } } }
	completion_reward_joint_originator = {
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = ALB }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				add_ideas = ALB_teskilat_i_mahsusa
				create_intelligence_agency = {
					name = "Teşkilât-ı Mahsusa"
					icon = GFX_intelligence_agency_logo_tur_ww1_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_done_agency_upgrade = upgrade_passive_defense } }
					upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_passive_defense
					set_country_flag = TUR_empower_the_special_organization
				}
			}
			else = { add_ideas = ALB_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = TUR }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa
					add_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_2
				}
			}
			else = {
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar
					add_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar_2
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = ALB_utilize_turkish_intelligence_tt
			hidden_effect = { set_division_template_cap = { division_template = "Teşkilât-ı Mahsusa Birliği" division_cap = 12 } }
		}
	}
	completion_reward_joint_member = {
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = ALB }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				add_ideas = ALB_teskilat_i_mahsusa
				create_intelligence_agency = {
					name = "Teşkilât-ı Mahsusa"
					icon = GFX_intelligence_agency_logo_tur_ww1_1
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_done_agency_upgrade = upgrade_passive_defense } }
					upgrade_intelligence_agency = upgrade_passive_defense
					set_country_flag = TUR_empower_the_special_organization
				}
			}
			else = { add_ideas = ALB_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = TUR }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa
					add_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_2
				}
			}
			else = {
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar
					add_idea = TUR_teskilat_i_mahsusa_no_lar_2
				}
			}
			set_division_template_cap = { division_template = "Teşkilât-ı Mahsusa Birliği" division_cap = 12 }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		modifier = {
			factor = 30
			original_tag = ALB
		}
	}
}
